Benchmarks either model did not run are omitted.",children:z.jsx(Vpe,{d:e,m:t,ref:i})}),z.jsxs("div",{className:"grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3",children:[e.areas.map(u=>z.jsx(B3,{d:e,m:t,title:u.label,score:(100*t.areas[u.id]).toFixed(1),bs:e.benchmarks.filter(c=>c.area===u.id)},u.id)),a.length>0&&z.jsx(B3,{d:e,m:t,title:"Reported, not indexed",score:null,bs:a})]}),z.jsxs(ei,{variant:"secondary",size:"xs",children:["Bar shows the chance-corrected score; value is the benchmark's own metric and rank among all ",e.models.length," entries. ★ marks gold benchmarks (weight 1.2).",(l=t.missing)!=null&&l.length?` Not run, scored 0: ${t.missing.map(u=>{var c;return((c=e.benchmarks.find(f=>f.id===u))==null?void 0:c.short)??u}).join(", ")}.`:""]})]})}function Ype({d:e}){const{pool:t}=lj(e),n=M.useMemo(()=>[...t].sort((u,c)=>c.index-u.index).slice(0,12),[t]),[r,i]=M.useState(()=>{const u=e.models.filter(h=>h.self_reported).map(h=>h.engine),c=e.models.filter(h=>h.closed).map(h=>h.engine),f=e.models.filter(h=>!h.self_reported&&!h.closed).sort((h,v)=>v.index-h.index).slice(0,2).map(h=>h.engine);return[...u,...c,...f]}),a=n.filter(u=>r.includes(u.engine)),l=t.filter(u=>u.ece==null).map(u=>u.name);return z.jsxs("div",{className:"flex flex-col gap-6",children:[z.jsx(ti,{title:"Quality vs latency, all models",description:z.jsxs(z.Fragment,{children:["Dashed line is the efficient frontier: no model is both faster and higher-scoring. Community latency is compute time on ",e.upstream.hardware,"; self-reported latency was measured on the authors' own stack and is not directly comparable."]}),children:z.jsx(kpe,{models:t})}),z.jsxs(ti,{title:"Area profiles",description:"Area scores for the selected models.",children:[z.jsx("div",{className:"mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2",children:n.map(u=>z.jsx(Ty,{label:u.name,checked:r.includes(u.engine),onCheckedChange:c=>i(f=>c?[...f,u.engine]:f.filter(h=>h!==u.engine))},u.engine))}),z.jsx(zpe,{d:e,models:a})]}),z.jsx(ti,{title:"Calibration vs quality",description:z.jsxs(z.Fragment,{children:["Expected calibration error as reported by the upstream board (lower is better).",l.length>0&&` Not shown (no ECE reported): ${l.join(", ")}.`]}),children:z.jsx(Bpe,{models:t})})]})}function Xpe({d:e}){const t=M.useMemo(()=>[...e.areas.flatMap(c=>e.benchmarks.filter(f=>f.area===c.id).map(f=>({value:String(f.id),label:`${IM[c.id]} · ${f.gold?"★ ":""}${f.short}`}))),...e.benchmarks.filter(c=>!c.in_index).map(c=>({value:String(c.id),label:`Not indexed · ${c.short}`}))],[e]),[n,r]=M.useState(t[0].value),i=e.benchmarks.find(c=>String(c.id)===n),a=e.areas.find(c=>c.id===i.area),l=e.models.filter(c=>c.scores[i.id]!=null).sort((c,f)=>i.lower?c.scores[i.id]-f.scores[i.id]:f.scores[i.id]-c.scores[i.id]),u=_f(l.map(c=>c.scores[i.id]),i.lower);return z.jsxs("div",{className:"flex flex-col gap-6",children:[z.jsx(hd,{"aria-label":"Benchmark",className:"w-80",value:n,onValueChange:c=>c&&r(c),items:t}),z.jsx(ti,{title:i.name,description:z.jsxs(z.Fragment,{children:[a?a.label:"Not in index"," · ",i.metric,i.lower?" (lower is better)":"",!i.lower&&i.chance!=null&&` · chance ${(100*i.chance).toFixed(1)}`,i.explainer?`. ${i.explainer}`:""]}),children:z.jsx(Hpe,{d:e,b:i})}),z.jsx(ti,{title:"All results",description:`${l.length} models`,flush:!0,children:z.jsxs(dt,{children:[z.jsx(dt.Header,{children:z.jsxs(dt.Row,{children:[z.jsx(dt.Head,{className:"w-10",children:"#"}),z.jsx(dt.Head,{children:"Model"}),z.jsx(dt.Head,{className:"text-right",children:"Score"}),z.jsx(dt.Head,{className:"text-right",children:"Chance-corrected"})]})}),z.jsx(dt.Body,{children:l.map((c,f)=>z.jsxs(dt.Row,{children:[z.jsx(dt.Cell,{className:"text-kumo-subtle",children:f+1}),z.jsx(dt.Cell,{children:z.jsxs("div",{className:"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2",children:[z.jsx(uj,{m:c}),z.jsx(oj,{m:c,d:e})]})}),z.jsx(dt.Cell,{className:`text-right ${u(c.scores[i.id])==="best"?"font-semibold":""}`,children:CG(i,c.scores[i.id])}),z.jsx(dt.Cell,{className:"text-right",children:c.skills[i.id]==null?z.jsx(nv,{}):(100*c.skills[i.id]).toFixed(1)})]},c.engine))})]})})]})}function qpe({d:e}){const t=z.jsx(Xo,{href:e.upstream.url,target:"_blank",rel:"noopener noreferrer",children:e.upstream.label});return z.jsxs("div",{className:"grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2",children:[z.jsx(ti,{title:"Scope",children:z.jsxs(ei,{children:["This page reproduces the community ",t," leaderboard and adds self-reported entries. It is not an exhaustive list of decision models: it covers only the models currently scored in the Decision Index, plus Cloudflare's Clef models. Every community result is taken unchanged from the upstream board's published data (snapshot ",rC(e.upstream.generated_utc),"). The index formula is re-implemented here and checked against every upstream model's published score at build time; the build fails if any disagree."]})}),z.jsx(ti,{title:"Self-reported entries",children:z.jsxs(ei,{children:["Entries marked ",z.jsx(Pu,{variant:"orange",children:"Self-reported"})," were run by the model's authors on the same benchmark suite and have not been reproduced by the upstream maintainers. Benchmarks an entry did not run are scored 0, following the upstream rule that unanswered requests count as wrong. Their latency was measured on the authors' own serving stack, not the upstream reference hardware (",e.upstream.hardware,"), and is not directly comparable."]})}),z.jsx(ti,{title:"Scoring",children:z.jsxs("div",{className:"flex flex-col gap-3",children:[z.jsxs(ei,{children:["Each benchmark score ",z.jsx("i",{children:"s"})," is corrected for chance level ",z.jsx("i",{children:"c"}),", so random guessing scores 0 and a perfect answer scores 1:"]}),z.jsx("code",{className:"self-start rounded-md bg-kumo-elevated px-3 py-2 font-mono text-sm",children:"k = clip((s − c) / (1 − c))"}),z.jsx(ei,{children:"ForecastBench (Brier loss) is converted as clip((0.25 − Brier) / 0.25). Each area is the weighted mean of its benchmarks, with gold ★ benchmarks weighted 1.2. The Decision Index is 100 × the weighted mean of the five areas."})]})}),z.jsx(ti,{title:"Acknowledgements",children:z.jsxs(ei,{children:["The benchmark suite, chance levels, area weights and all community results are the work of the ",t," maintainers and contributors. This page is unofficial and is not affiliated with them or with TypeSafe."]})}),z.jsx("div",{className:"lg:col-span-2",children:z.jsx(ti,{title:"Areas and weights",flush:!0,children:z.jsxs(dt,{children:[z.jsx(dt.Header,{children:z.jsxs(dt.Row,{children:[z.jsx(dt.Head,{children:"Area"}),z.jsx(dt.Head,{className:"text-right",children:"Weight"}),z.jsx(dt.Head,{children:"Benchmarks"})]})}),z.jsx(dt.Body,{children:e.areas.map(n=>z.jsxs(dt.Row,{children:[z.jsx(dt.Cell,{className:"font-medium",children:n.label}),z.jsxs(dt.Cell,{className:"text-right",children:[(100*n.weight).toFixed(1),"%"]}),z.jsx(dt.Cell,{className:"whitespace-normal",children:e.benchmarks.filter(r=>r.area===n.id).map(r=>(r.gold?"★ ":"")+r.short).join(", ")})]},n.id))})]})})})]})}document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-mode","light");sY.createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(z.jsx(M.StrictMode,{children:z.jsx(Fpe,{})}));
